import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, L1, L2, L3, isSunnyDec]
  have hcover : coversTriangle 3 lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum
    have : (a = 1 ∧ b = 1) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 3) ∨ (a = 2 ∧ b = 1) ∨
           (a = 2 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 3 ∧ b = 1) := by
      omega
    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 2*(n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := ns_lines ++ [S1, S2, S3]
  
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, S1, S2, S3, isSunnyDec, vlist_filter (n-3)]
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a ≤ n-3
    · have hm : vert a ∈ vlist (n-3) := vlist_mem a (n-3) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S2, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb1 : b = 1 := by omega
        subst hb1
        refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
        simp [S2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- Impossibility lemmas

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    linarith
  have hq_ne_zero : L.q ≠ 0 := hq
  have : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    linarith
  have hp_ne_zero : L.p ≠ 0 := hp
  have : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

lemma three_points_not_covered_by_two_sunny (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n)
    (lines : List Line) (hlines : lines.length = n)
    (hcovers : coversTriangle n lines) :
    (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length ≠ 2 := by
  intro hk2
  have h_has_11 : (1, 1) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    dsimp [TrianglePoints]
    have : 1 + 1 ≤ n + 1 := by omega
    exact ⟨by omega, by omega, this⟩
  have h_has_12 : (1, 2) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    dsimp [TrianglePoints]
    have : 1 + 2 ≤ n + 1 := by omega
    exact ⟨by omega, by omega, this⟩
  have h_has_21 : (2, 1) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    dsimp [TrianglePoints]
    have : 2 + 1 ≤ n + 1 := by omega
    exact ⟨by omega, by omega, this⟩
  rcases hcovers 1 1 h_has_11 with ⟨L11, mem11, lies11⟩
  rcases hcovers 1 2 h_has_12 with ⟨L12, mem12, lies12⟩
  rcases hcovers 2 1 h_has_21 with ⟨L21, mem21, lies21⟩
  sorry

theorem sunny_lines_main_theorem (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : ({0, 1, 3} : Set ℕ) ⊆ achievableKs n := by
  intro k hk; simp at hk
  rcases hk with (hk0|hk1|hk3)
  · subst hk0; exact k0_achievable n
  · subst hk1; exact k1_achievable n hn
  · subst hk3
    by_cases h : n = 3
    · subst h; exact k3_achievable_n3
    · have h4 : 4 ≤ n := by omega
      exact k3_achievable_nge4 n h4

end SunnyLines

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma isSunny_of_mem_filter (L : Line) (lines : List Line) (h : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines) : isSunny L := by
  rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩
  rw [isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny] at hdec
  exact hdec

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by

    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum

    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 2*(n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := ns_lines ++ [S1, S2, S3]

  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by

    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩

      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩

-- Impossibility lemmas - using mul_eq_zero.mp and Nat subtraction

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hq_ne_zero : L.q ≠ 0 := hq
  have : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hq0 | hsub)
    · exact (hq_ne_zero hq0).elim
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hp_ne_zero : L.p ≠ 0 := hp
  have : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hp0 | hsub)
    · exact (hp_ne_zero hp0).elim
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- The key impossibility result: 2 sunny lines cannot work
lemma three_points_not_covered_by_two_sunny (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n)
    (lines : List Line) (hlines : lines.length = n)
    (hcovers : coversTriangle n lines) :

    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, L1, L2, L3, isSunnyDec]
  have hcover : coversTriangle 3 lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum
    have : (a = 1 ∧ b = 1) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 3) ∨ (a = 2 ∧ b = 1) ∨
           (a = 2 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 3 ∧ b = 1) := by
      omega
    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 2*(n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := ns_lines ++ [S1, S2, S3]
  
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, S1, S2, S3, isSunnyDec, vlist_filter (n-3)]
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a ≤ n-3
    · have hm : vert a ∈ vlist (n-3) := vlist_mem a (n-3) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S2, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb1 : b = 1 := by omega
        subst hb1
        refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
        simp [S2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- Impossibility lemmas - using ring domain property for ℤ

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hq_ne_zero : L.q ≠ 0 := hq
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hq0 | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq_ne_zero hq0
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hp_ne_zero : L.p ≠ 0 := hp
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hp0 | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp_ne_zero hp0
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- A non-sunny line is one where q=0, p=0, or p=q
lemma nonSunny_cases (L : Line) (h : ¬ isSunny L) : L.q = 0 ∨ L.p = 0 ∨ L.p = L.q := by
  unfold isSunny at h
  simp at h
  exact h

  rcases hcovers 1 2 hp12 with ⟨L12, mem12, lies12⟩
  rcases hcovers 2 1 hp21 with ⟨L21, mem21, lies21⟩

    have h2 : ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) := by

    have h3 : ¬ (liesOn 1 2 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) := by

  -- But we have exactly 2 sunny lines, and the 3 points need to be covered
  -- If either L11, L12, L21 is sunny, it covers at most 1 of the 3 points
  -- The remaining points would need to be covered by non-sunny lines
  -- Non-sunny lines are: vertical (q=0), horizontal (p=0), or slope -1 (p=q)
  
  -- A vertical line x = c can only cover points with a specific x-coordinate
  -- A horizontal line y = c can only cover points with a specific y-coordinate

    by_cases h : L11 = L12; { left; exact h }
    by_cases h' : L11 = L21; { right; left; exact h' }
    by_cases h'' : L12 = L21; { right; right; left; exact h'' }

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma isSunny_of_mem_filter (L : Line) (lines : List Line) (h : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines) : isSunny L := by
  rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩
  rw [isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny] at hdec
  exact hdec

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

lemma mem_triangle_iff (a b n : ℕ) : (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n ↔ 1 ≤ a ∧ 1 ≤ b ∧ a + b ≤ n + 1 := by
  rfl

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)

  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- Three points (1,1), (1,2), (2,1) cannot all be covered if there are only 2 sunny lines
lemma three_points_not_covered_by_two_sunny (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n)

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma isSunny_of_mem_filter (L : Line) (lines : List Line) (h : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines) : isSunny L := by
  rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩
  rw [isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny] at hdec
  exact hdec

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, L1, L2, L3, isSunnyDec]
  have hcover : coversTriangle 3 lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum
    have : (a = 1 ∧ b = 1) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 3) ∨ (a = 2 ∧ b = 1) ∨
           (a = 2 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 3 ∧ b = 1) := by
      omega
    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by

      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩

    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega

        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩

-- Simple algebraic lemmas about sunny lines

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    refine mul_eq_zero.mp h_diff |>.resolve_left hq
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    refine mul_eq_zero.mp h_diff |>.resolve_left hp
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- Any line (sunny or not) covers at most one of the three points (1,1), (1,2), (2,1)
lemma at_most_one_of_three (L : Line) :
    ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 1 2 L) ∧
    ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) ∧
    ¬ (liesOn 1 2 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) := by
  by_cases h_sunny : isSunny L
  · have h1 : ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 1 2 L) := by
      rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
      have : 1 = 2 := sunny_not_same_x L h_sunny 1 1 2 h h'
      omega
    have h2 : ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) := by
      rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
      have : 1 = 2 := sunny_not_same_y L h_sunny 1 2 1 h h'
      omega
    have h3 : ¬ (liesOn 1 2 L ∧ liesOn 2 1 L) := by
      rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
      exact sunny_not_slope_neg_one L h_sunny 1 1 h h'
    exact ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩
  · -- L is not sunny: q=0 (vertical), p=0 (horizontal), or p=q (slope -1)
    unfold isSunny at h_sunny
    push_neg at h_sunny
    rcases h_sunny with (hq0|hp0|hpq)
    · -- Vertical line: q=0, so liesOn means L.p * a = L.r. Only one a works.
      have h1 : ¬ (liesOn 1 1 L ∧ liesOn 1 2 L) := by
        rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
        unfold liesOn at h h'
        simp [hq0] at h h'
        -- Both give L.p * 1 = L.r, so no contradiction with same a
        -- Actually vertical can cover (1,1) and (1,2) both with same x=1
        -- So this pair IS possible for vertical lines. But...
        -- We need a different approach.
        sorry
      sorry
    · sorry
    · sorry

end SunnyLines

If k ≤ 2, then at most 2 of the x-values in {1,...,n} can be partially covered by sunny lines. All other x-values must be completely covered by a single vertical line. Vertical line x=c is non-sunny (q=0). There are n-k non-sunny lines. So at most n-k x-values get vertical coverage. The remaining k x-values must be covered by sunny lines. But for any x-value with x ≤ n-1, there are at least 2 points with that x (since (x,1) and (x,2) are both in the triangle if x+2 ≤ n+1, i.e., x ≤ n-1). A sunny line covers at most 1 point with that x. So we need ≥ 2 sunny lines per x-value with x ≤ n-1. Therefore, with k=2, we can cover at most 1 x-value (x=n, with 1 point). For each x ∈ {1,...,n}, let v(x) = number of points in the triangle with that x-coordinate = n+1-x.

Case 1: x is covered by a vertical line. Then all v(x) points are covered by 1 non-sunny line.

Case 2: x is covered by sunny lines. Since each sunny line covers at most 1 point with that x, we need at least v(x) sunny lines that cover points at this x.

Total count: Let V be the set of x-values covered by vertical lines. For x ∈ V, we use 1 non-sunny line. For x ∉ V, we need at least v(x) sunny lines.

Total lines ≥ |V| + Σ_{x∉V} v(x) = |V| + Σ_{x∉V} (n+1-x)

With k=2 sunny lines: Σ_{x∉V} v(x) ≤ 2 (since each sunny line can contribute to at most 1 x-value)

Interesting! A sunny line typically covers at most about n/2 points. So with k sunny lines, we can cover at most about k * ceil(n/2) points. The total triangle has n(n+1)/2 points. With n-k non-sunny (vertical) lines each covering at most n points (one full column), the total coverage is at most (n-k)*n + k*ceil(n/2). For this to cover n(n+1)/2 points, we need...

For n=4, n(n+1)/2 = 10. With k=2, max coverage = (4-2)*4 + 2*2 = 8+4=12... that's enough!
But k=2 was still impossible computationally. The issue is that vertical lines overlap in coverage with sunny lines, so the simple counting overestimates.

Let me think about this more carefully and construct a proper rigorous argument.

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma isSunny_of_mem_filter (L : Line) (lines : List Line) (h : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines) : isSunny L := by
  rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩
  rw [isSunnyDec_iff_isSunny] at hdec
  exact hdec

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter (n-1), sun1_isSunnyDec n]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, L1, L2, L3, isSunnyDec]
  have hcover : coversTriangle 3 lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum
    have : (a = 1 ∧ b = 1) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 3) ∨ (a = 2 ∧ b = 1) ∨
           (a = 2 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 3 ∧ b = 1) := by
      omega
    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 2*(n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := ns_lines ++ [S1, S2, S3]
  
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, S1, S2, S3, isSunnyDec, vlist_filter (n-3)]
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a ≤ n-3
    · have hm : vert a ∈ vlist (n-3) := vlist_mem a (n-3) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S2, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb1 : b = 1 := by omega
        subst hb1
        refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
        simp [S2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- Simple algebraic lemmas about sunny lines

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- Impossibility of k=2
-- We show that with 2 sunny lines among n lines, the triangle cannot be covered.
-- Strategy: each sunny line covers at most 1 point with a given x-coordinate.
-- For x in {1,...,n-1}, there are at least 2 points at that x: (x,1) and (x,2).
-- So covering these 2 points requires either:
--   (a) a vertical non-sunny line x=c, or
--   (b) 2 different sunny lines each covering one of them.
-- With only 2 sunny lines, at most one x-value can be covered by method (b).
-- All other x-values need vertical lines.

lemma at_most_one_per_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a : ℕ) :
    ∀ (b1 b2 : ℕ), liesOn a b1 L → liesOn a b2 L → b1 = b2 := by
  intro b1 b2 h1 h2
  exact sunny_not_same_x L h a b1 b2 h1 h2

lemma two_points_same_x_need_two_sunny_or_vertical (a : ℕ) (lines : List Line)
    (hcover : coversTriangle (a+1) lines) (hpos : a ≥ 1) : 
    (∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a 1 L ∧ liesOn a 2 L) ∨ 
    (∃ L1 ∈ lines, ∃ L2 ∈ lines, L1 ≠ L2 ∧ liesOn a 1 L1 ∧ liesOn a 2 L2) := by
  sorry

lemma impossible_k2 (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) (lines : List Line) (hlines : lines.length = n)
    (hcovers : coversTriangle n lines) : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length ≠ 2 := by
  intro hk2
  have h_nonempty : lines ≠ [] := by
    intro h; rw [h] at hlines; simp at hlines; omega
  -- Step 1: find all sunny lines
  let sunny_lines := List.filter isSunnyDec lines
  have h_sunny_len : sunny_lines.length = 2 := hk2
  have h_sunny_nonempty : sunny_lines ≠ [] := by
    intro h; rw [h] at h_sunny_len; simp at h_sunny_len
  -- Step 2: get the two sunny lines S1, S2
  have h_sunny_list : ∃ (S1 S2 : Line), S1 ≠ S2 ∧ sunny_lines = [S1, S2] := by
    sorry
  rcases h_sunny_list with ⟨S1, S2, hS_ne, hS_list⟩
  have hS1_sunny : isSunny S1 := isSunny_of_mem_filter S1 lines (by
    rw [hS_list]; simp)
  have hS2_sunny : isSunny S2 := isSunny_of_mem_filter S2 lines (by
    rw [hS_list]; simp)
  -- Step 3: key counting argument
  -- Points (n-1,1) and (n-1,2) are in the triangle (since n-1+2 = n+1 ≤ n+1)
  -- A sunny line can cover at most 1 of these

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

theorem isSunny_iff (L : Line) : isSunny L ↔ (L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q) := by rfl

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

lemma mem_TrianglePoints_iff (a b n : ℕ) : (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n ↔ 1 ≤ a ∧ 1 ≤ b ∧ a + b ≤ n + 1 := by rfl

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunnyDec (c : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (vert c) = false := by
  unfold isSunnyDec vert; simp

lemma vert_isSunny (c : ℕ) : ¬ isSunny (vert c) := by
  unfold isSunny vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunnyDec (n : ℕ) : isSunnyDec (sun1 n) = true := by
  unfold isSunnyDec sun1; simp

lemma sun1_isSunny (n : ℕ) : isSunny (sun1 n) := by
  unfold isSunny sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunnyDec, ih]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by nlinarith
  exact hneq h_eq

-- A sunny line covers at most 1 point per x-coordinate
lemma sunny_at_most_one_per_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a : ℕ) (b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 :=
  sunny_not_same_x L h a b1 b2 h1 h2

end SunnyLines

    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => rw [vlist, List.filter_append]; simp [vert_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_eq : L.p = L.q := by
    -- Subtract the two equations: (p*a + q*(b+1)) - (p*(a+1) + q*b) = 0
    -- => p*a + q*b + q - p*a - p - q*b = 0 => q - p = 0 => p = q
    have hcalc : L.q - L.p = 0 := by nlinarith
    omega
  exact hneq h_eq

end SunnyLines

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

lemma not_isSunny_of_mem_vlist (n a : ℕ) (h : a ∈ vlist n) : ¬ isSunny a := by
  sorry

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunny : ¬ isSunny (vert c) := by
  unfold isSunny vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunny : isSunny (sun1 n) := by
  unfold isSunny sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [vlist, List.filter_append]
    simp [vert_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff, ih]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have hcalc : L.p = L.q := by
    have htemp : L.q = L.p := by nlinarith
    omega
  exact hneq hcalc

end SunnyLines

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  -- h1: L.p*(a:ℤ) + L.q*(b+1:ℤ) = L.r
  -- h2: L.p*(a+1:ℤ) + L.q*(b:ℤ) = L.r
  -- Subtract h2 - h1: (L.p*(a+1) + L.q*b) - (L.p*a + L.q*(b+1)) = 0
  -- Simplify: L.p*a + L.p + L.q*b - L.p*a - L.q*b - L.q = 0 → L.p - L.q = 0 → L.p = L.q
  have hcalc : L.p - L.q = 0 := by
    nlinarith
  have heq : L.p = L.q := by omega
  exact hneq heq

-- k=0 achievable
theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

end SunnyLines

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

lemma isSunny_iff (L : Line) : isSunny L ↔ (L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q) := ⟨λ h => h, λ h => h⟩

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunny (c : ℕ) : ¬ isSunny (vert c) := by
  unfold isSunny vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunny (n : ℕ) : isSunny (sun1 n) := by
  unfold isSunny sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [vlist, List.filter_append]
    simp [vert_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff, ih]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  -- h1: L.p*(a:ℤ) + L.q*((b+1):ℤ) = L.r
  -- h2: L.p*((a+1):ℤ) + L.q*(b:ℤ) = L.r
  -- From h1: L.r = L.p*a + L.q*(b+1)
  -- From h2: L.r = L.p*(a+1) + L.q*b
  -- So: L.p*a + L.q*(b+1) = L.p*(a+1) + L.q*b
  -- Expand: L.p*a + L.q*b + L.q = L.p*a + L.p + L.q*b
  -- Cancel: L.q = L.p
  have hcalc : L.q = L.p := by
    linarith
  exact hneq hcalc.symm

-- k=0 achievable
theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

end SunnyLines

      _ = L.p := by ring
  exact hneq hcalc

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

lemma isSunny_iff (L : Line) : isSunny L ↔ (L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q) := ⟨λ h => h, λ h => h⟩

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunny (c : ℕ) : ¬ isSunny (vert c) := by
  unfold isSunny vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunny (n : ℕ) : isSunny (sun1 n) := by
  unfold isSunny sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [vlist, List.filter_append]
    simp [vert_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff, ih]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  -- h1: L.p*(a:ℤ) + L.q*((b+1):ℤ) = L.r
  -- h2: L.p*((a+1):ℤ) + L.q*(b:ℤ) = L.r
  -- So: L.p*a + L.q*(b+1) = L.p*(a+1) + L.q*b
  calc
    L.p = (L.p * ((a:ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by ring
    _ = L.r - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by rw [h2]
    _ = (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * ((b:ℤ) + 1)) - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by rw [h1]
    _ = L.q := by ring
  = L.q := rfl
  -- The calc ends at L.p = L.q
  have hcalc : L.p = L.q := by
    calc
      L.p = (L.p * ((a:ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by ring
      _ = L.r - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by rw [h2]
      _ = (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * ((b:ℤ) + 1)) - (L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * (b:ℤ)) := by rw [h1]
      _ = L.q := by ring
  exact hneq hcalc

end SunnyLines

## Live Stream

Let me fix this simply. The issue is that `liesOn` uses `(b : ℤ)` in the expression `L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r`, and when we say `liesOn a (b+1) L`, the second argument is `(b+1 : ℕ)`, and then the `liesOn` definition converts it to `(b+1 : ℤ)`. Meanwhile `L.q * ((b:ℤ) + 1)` is different from `L.q * ((b+1:ℤ))` for rewriting.

The simplest fix: don't use `calc` or `ring`, just use `nlinarith` directly with the right inputs.

`nlinarith` works when everything is in ℤ directly! The issue before was that `liesOn` gives `L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r` where `(a:ℤ)` is a coercion from ℕ. And `(b+1 : ℕ)` becomes `(↑(b+1) : ℤ)` which is `(b:ℤ) + 1`. So the equations are actually:
- `h1`: `L.p * (a:ℤ) + L.q * ((b:ℤ) + 1) = L.r`  (because `(b+1 : ℕ) ↦ (b:ℤ)+1`)
- `h2`: `L.p * ((a:ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b:ℤ) = L.r`

Wait, but the error showed `L.p * ↑a + L.q * ↑(b + 1) = L.r` not `L.q * (↑b + 1)`. So `↑(b+1)` is `(b+1:ℤ)` which is the same as `(b:ℤ) + 1` by `Nat.cast_add`. So the rewrite should work.

Let me just use `nlinarith` directly on the unfolded equations:

import Mathlib

example (p q : ℤ) (a b : ℕ) (r : ℤ) (h1 : p*(a:ℤ) + q*((b+1:ℕ):ℤ) = r) (h2 : p*((a+1:ℕ):ℤ) + q*(b:ℤ) = r) : q = p := by
  -- Convert the casts
  have h1' : p*(a:ℤ) + q*((b:ℤ) + 1) = r := by simpa using h1
  have h2' : p*((a:ℤ) + 1) + q*(b:ℤ) = r := by simpa using h2

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have hcalc : L.q = L.p := by
    have h1' : L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * ((b : ℤ) + 1) = L.r := by simpa using h1
    have h2' : L.p * ((a : ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r := by simpa using h2
    nlinarith
  exact hneq hcalc.symm

-- k=0 achievable
theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

end SunnyLines

import Mathlib

open Set

namespace SunnyLines

structure Line : Type where
  (p q r : ℤ)
  h_nonzero : p ≠ 0 ∨ q ≠ 0

def isSunny (L : Line) : Prop := L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q

lemma isSunny_iff (L : Line) : isSunny L ↔ (L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q) := ⟨λ h => h, λ h => h⟩

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp [and_assoc]

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

lemma mem_TrianglePoints_iff (a b n : ℕ) : (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n ↔ 1 ≤ a ∧ 1 ≤ b ∧ a + b ≤ n + 1 := by rfl

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

def achievableKs (n : ℕ) : Set ℕ :=
  {k | ∃ (lines : List Line), lines.length = n ∧ coversTriangle n lines ∧
       (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = k}

def vert (c : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, 0, (c : ℤ), Or.inl (by norm_num : (1 : ℤ) ≠ 0)⟩

lemma vert_isSunny (c : ℕ) : ¬ isSunny (vert c) := by
  unfold isSunny vert; simp

lemma vert_liesOn (a b c : ℕ) : liesOn a b (vert c) ↔ a = c := by
  unfold liesOn vert; simp

def sun1 (n : ℕ) : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩

lemma sun1_isSunny (n : ℕ) : isSunny (sun1 n) := by
  unfold isSunny sun1; simp

def vlist : ℕ → List Line
  | 0 => []
  | n+1 => vlist n ++ [vert (n+1)]

lemma vlist_len (n : ℕ) : (vlist n).length = n := by
  induction n with | zero => rfl | succ n ih => simp [vlist, ih]

lemma vlist_mem (a n : ℕ) (hpos : 1 ≤ a) (hle : a ≤ n) : vert a ∈ vlist n := by
  revert a; induction n with
  | zero => intro a hpos hle; omega
  | succ n ih =>
    intro a hpos hle; rw [vlist]
    by_cases ha : a = n+1
    · subst a; simp
    · have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
      apply List.mem_append_left; exact ih a hpos ha'

lemma vlist_filter (n : ℕ) : (List.filter isSunnyDec (vlist n)).length = 0 := by
  induction n with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [vlist, List.filter_append]
    simp [vert_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff, ih]

-- Sunny line constraints

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have hcalc : L.q = L.p := by
    have h1' : L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * ((b : ℤ) + 1) = L.r := by simpa using h1
    have h2' : L.p * ((a : ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r := by simpa using h2
    nlinarith
  exact hneq hcalc.symm

-- k=0 achievable
theorem k0_achievable (n : ℕ) : (0 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  refine ⟨vlist n, vlist_len n, ?_, vlist_filter n⟩
  intro a b h; rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
  have ha' : a ≤ n := by omega
  refine ⟨vert a, vlist_mem a n ha ha', ?_⟩
  rw [vert_liesOn]

-- k=1 achievable
theorem k1_achievable (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : (1 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let lines := vlist (n-1) ++ [sun1 n]
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a' b' h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a' ≤ n-1
    · have hm : vert a' ∈ vlist (n-1) := vlist_mem a' (n-1) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a', List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have han : a' = n := by omega
      have hb1 : b' = 1 := by omega
      rw [han, hb1]
      refine ⟨sun1 n, ?_, ?_⟩
      · simp [lines]
      · unfold liesOn sun1; simp [sub_eq_add_neg]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 1 := by
    simp [lines, vlist_filter, sun1_isSunny, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- k=3 achievable for n=3
theorem k3_achievable_n3 : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs 3 := by
  let L1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, 0, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let L3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 5, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := [L1, L2, L3]
  have hlen : lines.length = 3 := by simp [lines]
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, L1, L2, L3, isSunnyDec, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff]
  have hcover : coversTriangle 3 lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    have hsum' : a + b ≤ 4 := hsum
    have : (a = 1 ∧ b = 1) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 1 ∧ b = 3) ∨ (a = 2 ∧ b = 1) ∨
           (a = 2 ∧ b = 2) ∨ (a = 3 ∧ b = 1) := by
      omega
    rcases this with (⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩|⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L3, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L3, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L1, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L1, liesOn]
    · refine ⟨L2, by simp [lines], ?_⟩; simp [L2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- k=3 achievable for n >= 4
theorem k3_achievable_nge4 (n : ℕ) (hn : 4 ≤ n) : (3 : ℕ) ∈ achievableKs n := by
  let ns_lines := vlist (n-3)
  let S1 : Line := ⟨1, -1, (n : ℤ) - 3, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S2 : Line := ⟨1, 2, (n : ℤ) + 2, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let S3 : Line := ⟨2, 1, 2*(n : ℤ) - 1, Or.inl (by norm_num)⟩
  let lines := ns_lines ++ [S1, S2, S3]
  
  have hlen : lines.length = n := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, vlist_len]; omega
  have hfilter : (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length = 3 := by
    simp [lines, ns_lines, S1, S2, S3, isSunnyDec, isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff, vlist_filter]
  have hcover : coversTriangle n lines := by
    intro a b h
    rcases h with ⟨ha, hb, hsum⟩
    by_cases ha_small : a ≤ n-3
    · have hm : vert a ∈ vlist (n-3) := vlist_mem a (n-3) ha ha_small
      refine ⟨vert a, List.mem_append_left _ hm, ?_⟩
      rw [vert_liesOn]
    · have ha_cases : a = n-2 ∨ a = n-1 ∨ a = n := by omega
      rcases ha_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 ∨ b = 3 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S2, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb_cases : b = 1 ∨ b = 2 := by omega
        rcases hb_cases with (rfl|rfl)
        · refine ⟨S3, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S3, liesOn]; omega
        · refine ⟨S1, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
          simp [S1, liesOn]; omega
      · have hb1 : b = 1 := by omega
        subst hb1
        refine ⟨S2, by simp [lines, ns_lines], ?_⟩
        simp [S2, liesOn]
  exact ⟨lines, hlen, hcover, hfilter⟩

-- Impossibility of k=2 - placeholder for worker sub_6
lemma three_points_not_covered_by_two_sunny (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n)
    (lines : List Line) (hlines : lines.length = n)
    (hcovers : coversTriangle n lines) :
    (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length ≠ 2 := by
  sorry

-- Main theorem: {0, 1, 3} ⊆ achievableKs(n) for all n ≥ 3
theorem sunny_lines_theorem (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n) : ({0, 1, 3} : Set ℕ) ⊆ achievableKs n := by
  intro k hk; simp at hk
  rcases hk with (hk0|hk1|hk3)
  · subst hk0; exact k0_achievable n
  · subst hk1; exact k1_achievable n hn
  · subst hk3
    by_cases h : n = 3
    · subst h; exact k3_achievable_n3
    · have h4 : 4 ≤ n := by omega
      exact k3_achievable_nge4 n h4

end SunnyLines

## Live Stream

Let me clean up the warnings and write the impossibility lemma myself while waiting for the workers. The cleanest approach for the impossibility lemma:

**Proof that k=2 is impossible:**

For any n ≥ 3, the triangle includes points (n-1,1), (n-1,2), (n,1).
- A sunny line covers at most 1 point with x=n-1 (by `sunny_not_same_x`).
- So covering both (n-1,1) and (n-1,2) requires either a vertical line x=n-1 (non-sunny) or two different sunny lines.
- Point (n,1) also needs coverage. If vertical x=n-1 exists, we need another line for (n,1).
- With only 2 sunny lines, we can cover at most: 2 points with x=n-1 (using both sunny lines) + 0 for x=n. Then (n,1) is uncovered.
- Or: 1 point with x=n-1 (1 sunny) + 1 point with x=n (other sunny). Then (n-1,2) or (n-1,1) is uncovered.
- Non-sunny lines: vertical x=c covers all at x=c. Horizontal y=c covers one per x. Slope -1 covers at most 2 specific points.
- Any way we arrange it, 3 points need coverage from what amounts to 3 separate "resources."

lemma isSunny_iff (L : Line) : isSunny L ↔ (L.q ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ 0 ∧ L.p ≠ L.q) := ⟨λ h => h, λ h => h⟩

def isSunnyDec (L : Line) : Bool := L.q != 0 && L.p != 0 && L.p != L.q

lemma isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff (L : Line) : isSunnyDec L = true ↔ isSunny L := by
  unfold isSunnyDec isSunny
  simp [and_assoc]

lemma mem_filter_isSunnyDec (L : Line) (lines : List Line) : L ∈ List.filter isSunnyDec lines ↔ (L ∈ lines ∧ isSunny L) := by
  constructor
  · intro h; rcases List.mem_filter.mp h with ⟨hmem, hdec⟩; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mp hdec⟩
  · intro ⟨hmem, hsunny⟩; apply List.mem_filter.mpr; exact ⟨hmem, (isSunnyDec_eq_true_iff L).mpr hsunny⟩

lemma sunny_not_same_x (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b1 b2 : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a b1 L) (h2 : liesOn a b2 L) : b1 = b2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.q * ((b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (b1 : ℤ) - (b2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hqz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hq hqz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_same_y (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a1 a2 b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a1 b L) (h2 : liesOn a2 b L) : a1 = a2 := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have h_diff : L.p * ((a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ)) = 0 := by nlinarith
  have hzero : (a1 : ℤ) - (a2 : ℤ) = 0 := by
    apply mul_eq_zero.mp at h_diff
    rcases h_diff with (hpz | hsub)
    · exfalso; exact hp hpz
    · exact hsub
  omega

lemma sunny_not_slope_neg_one (L : Line) (h : isSunny L) (a b : ℕ)
    (h1 : liesOn a (b+1) L) (h2 : liesOn (a+1) b L) : False := by
  rcases h with ⟨hq, hp, hneq⟩
  unfold liesOn at h1 h2
  have hcalc : L.q = L.p := by
    have h1' : L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * ((b : ℤ) + 1) = L.r := by simpa using h1
    have h2' : L.p * ((a : ℤ) + 1) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r := by simpa using h2
    nlinarith
  exact hneq hcalc.symm

def liesOn (a b : ℕ) (L : Line) : Prop := L.p * (a : ℤ) + L.q * (b : ℤ) = L.r

def TrianglePoints (n : ℕ) : Set (ℕ × ℕ) := {p | 1 ≤ p.1 ∧ 1 ≤ p.2 ∧ p.1 + p.2 ≤ n + 1}

def coversTriangle (n : ℕ) (lines : List Line) : Prop :=
  ∀ (a b : ℕ), (a, b) ∈ TrianglePoints n → ∃ L ∈ lines, liesOn a b L

-- Impossibility of k=2
lemma three_points_not_covered_by_two_sunny (n : ℕ) (hn : 3 ≤ n)
    (lines : List Line) (hlines : lines.length = n)
    (hcovers : coversTriangle n lines) :
    (List.filter isSunnyDec lines).length ≠ 2 := by
  intro hk2
  -- The triangle contains (n-1,1), (n-1,2), (n,1) for all n ≥ 3
  have hp1 : (n-1, 1) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    refine ⟨by omega, by omega, ?_⟩
    have : (n-1) + 1 = n := by omega
    omega
  have hp2 : (n-1, 2) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    refine ⟨by omega, by omega, ?_⟩
    have : (n-1) + 2 = n+1 := by omega
    omega
  have hp3 : (n, 1) ∈ TrianglePoints n := by
    refine ⟨by omega, by omega, ?_⟩
    omega
  rcases hcovers (n-1) 1 hp1 with ⟨L1, mem1, lies1⟩
  rcases hcovers (n-1) 2 hp2 with ⟨L2, mem2, lies2⟩
  rcases hcovers n 1 hp3 with ⟨L3, mem3, lies3⟩
  
  let sunny_lines := List.filter isSunnyDec lines
  have h_sunny_len : sunny_lines.length = 2 := hk2
  
  -- Key observation: a sunny line can contain at most one of (n-1,1) and (n-1,2)
  -- because they share the same x-coordinate n-1.
  -- So if L1 or L2 is sunny, it cannot cover both.
  
  -- Consider cases based on which of L1, L2, L3 are sunny.
  by_cases hL1_sunny : L1 ∈ sunny_lines
  · by_cases hL2_sunny : L2 ∈ sunny_lines
    · -- L1 and L2 are sunny and distinct (or same)
      by_cases h_eq : L1 = L2
      · -- Same sunny line covers (n-1,1) and (n-1,2) - impossible
        subst h_eq
        have h_sunny : isSunny L1 := (mem_filter_isSunnyDec L1 lines).mp hL1_sunny |>.2
        have : 1 = 2 := sunny_not_same_x L1 h_sunny (n-1) 1 2 lies1 lies2
        omega
      · -- L1 and L2 are distinct sunny lines covering (n-1,1) and (n-1,2)
        -- Then L3 must cover (n,1). L3 could be L1, L2, or a third line.
        by_cases hL3_sunny : L3 ∈ sunny_lines

Let me write the complete Lean code with a clean impossibility proof. The key insight:

For any line L covering (n-1,1) and (n-1,2), either:
1. L is sunny → impossible by `sunny_not_same_x` (same x)
2. L is non-sunny → L is vertical x=n-1 (since only vertical lines cover same-x points; horizontal and slope -1 lines can't cover two points with same x unless the two points have different y... wait, a horizontal line y=1 covers (n-1,1) but NOT (n-1,2). A slope -1 line x-y=n-2 covers (n-1,1) but NOT (n-1,2).)

So if (n-1,1) and (n-1,2) are covered by the SAME line, that line must be vertical x=n-1. If they're covered by DIFFERENT lines, then each must be either sunny (covering at most 1 of the pair) or non-sunny (vertical x=n-1).

With only 2 sunny lines total, and multiple columns to cover, the counting argument shows impossibility.
